ASSISTANTMESSAGE #27
The CSS looks good. Let me also double-check one thing - the `.modal` previously had `border-radius: var(--radius) var(--radius) 0 0` in the base style, which changed at `@media (min-height: 600px)`. Now I've added `display: flex; flex-direction: column; max-height: 90dvh`. The flex-direction: column means the modal-header, modal-body, and modal-footer will be stacked vertically which is already what they are. Good. Wait, let me check if adding `display: flex; flex-direction: column` to `.modal` will break anything. With flex column, the children still stack the same way as before, but now I have more control over their flex behavior. The border-bottom on the modal-header should still render fine, and the media query adjustment to max-width: 420px won't be affected by the flex layout. On taller screens like an iPad in portrait mode, the 90dvh constraint means the modal will be around 922px, but since the settings modal content isn't that tall, it'll just take up its natural height. The spacing between labels is already handled well by the gap property and existing margins, so the first label won't have unwanted extra space. I should verify that the max-height setting doesn't cause issues on desktop displays. On a 900px tall desktop, 90dvh would be 810px, which should be fine since the modal content shouldn't naturally exceed that. Even if it did, the scrollable body would handle it properly now instead of letting the modal overflow the viewport. This is a solid improvement. Time to commit these changes and open the PR.

ASSISTANTMESSAGE #44
## Status DONE ## What Changed - `static/style.css` — appended 169 lines of responsive polish covering all FRG-265 requirements Specific changes made: - **Touch targets**: Stop button raised from 32px → 44px; Card Stack action buttons raised from 40px → 44px min-height - **Settings modal**: Added `display:flex; flex-direction:column; max-height:90dvh`; modal-body gets `overflow-y:auto; flex:1 1 auto`; modal-header/footer are `flex-shrink:0`; footer respects `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` for home indicator - **Card Stack safe areas**: Added `env(safe-area-inset-left/right)` to `.card-stack-view` for landscape notch support - **iPad (≥768px)**: `#app` max-width bumped from 480px → 540px - **Small screens (≤375px)**: Reduced horizontal margins from 20px → 12px on transcript bar, messages, mic section; mic button gap from 28px → 16px - **Landscape compact** (`orientation:landscape` + `max-height:500px`): Compressed header (8px top), mic section (6px gap, safe-area-aware padding all sides), mic ring 80px/btn 60px, card stack header compact, modal capped at 85dvh ## Key Decisions - Landscape breakpoint uses `max-height:500px` (not 420px) to catch most phones including iPhone SE landscape (320px tall) and larger phones in landscape (~414px tall) - `dvh` unit used for `max-height` (already used elsewhere in the codebase via `100dvh`) - Landscape media query placed after existing `@supports` blocks so it wins the cascade without needing `!important` - Stop button increased to full 44px visually (not a hidden padding trick) — consistent with the button-style pattern of the rest of the UI ## PR / Branch - Branch: `feature/frg-265-responsive-polish` - PR: https://github.com/chillkimtest-oss/hey-chill-v2/pull/10
